Coordinate descent detailed implementation:
```
def _coord_descent(a, deadline, cycles=50, verbose=True, candidates=120):
    a = np.asarray(a, float).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return ...
    a /= s
    n = len(a); L=2*n-1
    C = _fft_convolve(a)
    C = np.maximum(C,0.0) # due roundoff
    best = _score_fast(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    # candidate u arrays relative to sB
    u_zero = 0.0
    # coarse u: log + linear
    u_coarse = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.linspace(0, 0.2, 30), np.logspace(np.log10(1e-5), np.log10(1.0), candidates)])
    u_coarse = np.clip(u_coarse,0,None); unique perhaps.
    # Could sort
    for cycle in range(cycles):
       if time>deadline break
       sum improvement=0; current_sum = a.sum() maybe scale to1 at cycle start.
       # randomize coordinate order? Could use fixed or random to escape.
       for i in range(n):
          old = a[i]
          sB = current_sum - old  # current_sum not 1? after updates no renorm
          if old == 0 and maybe? still.
          # Build L_B
          Lvec = np.zeros(L)
          # first shift i: k=i..i+n-1
          # We need B = a with a[i]=0. Use a slices, subtract self later.
          if i + n <= L:
              Lvec[i:i+n] += a
          start = max(0, i-n+1)
          end = min(i, L-1)
          if start <= end:
              # j = i - k; when k=start => j=i-start, k=end=i =>j=0
              segment = a[i-start:i+1][::-1]
              Lvec[start:end+1] += segment
          di = 2*i
          if di < L:
              Lvec[di] -= 2.0*old
          # C_B
          CB = C - old * Lvec
          if di < L:
              CB[di] -= old*old
          CB = np.maximum(CB, 0) # roundoff? But subtract may create tiny neg.
          denom = sB
          # If denom small (all mass at i), set x? skip.
          # Evaluate candidate u (x=sB*u)
          X = sB * u_coarse  # if sB zero? handle
          # but if sB small relative? Normalize a at cycle start to 1; sB ~1.
          # Compute scores
          # To avoid large memory, evaluate chunks if needed.
          maxvals = np.max(CB + X[:,None]*Lvec, axis=1)
          if di < L: maxvals = np.maximum(maxvals, CB[di] + X*X) # Wait for coordinate with di, CB[di]+X^2 is one coefficient; max over others already maybe. This is cheaper than adding to all.
          scores = maxvals / (denom + X)**2
          # But for X=0, denom sB.
          # Choose best u
          idx = argmin scores
          xu = X[idx]
          # Refine around xu
          if xu > 0:
              lower = max(0.0, xu/4)
              upper = xu*4
              # also include maybe broader if near bounds
              u_fine = np.linspace(lower/sB, upper/sB, 60) if sB>0 else ...
              # include log around
              # if lower<1e-12 add 0
              Xf = sB * u_fine; append 0,xu
              evaluate similar; choose best
          else:
              # maybe test very small linear to see if increasing helps
              Xf = sB * np.linspace(0, 0.05/candidates?); choose
          # Update if better in score (not just m)
          # New score = best m *2n
          m_new = min score *2n? Actually `_score_fast` uses 2n*max/(sum^2); m = max/(sum^2). We computed maxvals/(denom+X)^2 = m. Score=2n*m.
          if 2n*m_new < best + 1e-12:
              update a[i]=xu; current_sum += xu-old; C = CB + xu*Lvec + xu^2 δ (exact update) perhaps use max(C,0)
              if score improved: track
       if cycle %? print
       # Renormalize a to sum1? C scales by (1/s)^2. We can scale a and C to sum1 to maintain precision.
       s_cur=a.sum()
       if s_cur>0:
          a=a/s_cur
          C=C/(s_cur*s_cur)
       current_sum=1.0
    return best_a, best
```

Implement coordinate scoring function to avoid repeated code:
```
def _score_candidate_x(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X):
    # X np array
    # if lenX !=0
    # Use chunks to avoid memory? implement simple full.
    vals = CB + np.outer(X, Lvec)  # shape (M,L)
    if di is not None:
        self = CB[di] + X*X
        maxc = np.maximum(np.max(vals, axis=1), self)
    else:
        maxc = np.max(vals, axis=1)
    denom = (sB+X)**2
    return maxc / denom
```
But `CB + outer` broadcasting creates array. If M=0, handle.

def _coord_descent(a_init, deadline, max_cycles=100, candidates=120, refine_points=100, recompute_every=4, seed=1):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    C=_fft_convolve(a); C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    s=a.sum() # 1
    cur_score=_score_fast(a)
    best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # build coarse u based on n
    u0 = 1.0/n
    lo_log = max(1e-7, u0*1e-5)
    hi_log = 5.0
    # coarse: log + linear around 0..min(1, 20*u0)
    n_log=60; n_lin=60
    u_coarse = np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], np.logspace(np.log10(lo_log), np.log10(hi_log), n_log), np.linspace(0.0, min(1.0, 20*u0), n_lin))))
    # But if n small hi maybe? ok.
    for cycle in range(max_cycles):
       if time>deadline: break
       # maybe random order
       order = list(range(n)); rng.shuffle(order)
       improved_this_cycle=0
       for i in order:
          if time>deadline: break
          old=a[i]
          sB=s-old
          if sB <= 1e-14:
             # if all mass here, maybe split? create small neighbor? skip
             continue
          Lvec = np.zeros(L)
          # Lvec[i:i+n] = 2*a
          Lvec[i:i+n] = 2.0 * a
          di = 2*i
          if di < L:
             Lvec[di] = 0.0
          # But if old=0, setting Lvec[di]=0 removes 0? Since a[i]=0, okay. For other j? L includes 2a for all j; at self j=i zero. For i=0, di=0 set 0.
          CB = C - old * Lvec
          if di < L:
              CB[di] -= old*old
          # clip tiny negative
          # We can avoid full max clip if slow? C length ~1000, ok.
          CB = np.maximum(CB, 0.0)
          # Evaluate
          X = sB * u_coarse
          # If old is very out of coarse range? Add old/sB
          X_old = np.array([old])
          # Combine and unique? old maybe duplicates.
          # Use helper to evaluate combined; but if too many points, filter.
          X_all = np.concatenate((X, [old]))
          # sort unique perhaps
          # For speed, use coarse + old.
          m_all = _coord_scores(CB,Lvec,di,sB,X_all)
          idx = int(np.argmin(m_all))
          x_best = X_all[idx]
          m_best = m_all[idx]
          # refine if x_best >0
          if x_best > 1e-8:
              # local grid x around factor 0.3-3, ~80 points; plus log maybe
              lo = max(0.0, x_best*0.3)
              hi = x_best*3.0
              if hi <= lo: hi = lo + sB*1e-6
              # linear 60 and log 40
              u_lo = lo/sB; u_hi=hi/sB
              u_fine = np.concatenate((np.linspace(u_lo,u_hi,60), np.logspace(np.log10(max(u_lo,1e-9)), np.log10(max(u_hi,1e-9)),40)))
              Xf = sB*u_fine; Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, [x_best, old]))
              mf = _coord_scores(CB,Lvec,di,sB,Xf)
              idx2 = int(np.argmin(mf))
              if mf[idx2] < m_best:
                  x_best=Xf[idx2]; m_best=mf[idx2]
              # if m_best near coarse best, do ultra-fine around best ratio 0.85-1.15 (30)
              if x_best > 1e-8:
                  u_l = max(0.0, (x_best*0.85)/sB)
                  u_h = (x_best*1.15)/sB
                  Xuf = np.linspace(u_l,u_h,30)
                  Xuf = np.concatenate((Xuf,[x_best]))
                  muf = _coord_scores(CB,Lvec,di,sB,Xuf)
                  idx3=argmin
                  x_best=...
          else:
              # if zero, maybe check small positive grid 0..max(u0*10,1e-4)
              Xf = sB*np.linspace(0, max(u0*20,1e-4), 30)
              ...
          new_score=2*n*m_best
          if new_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
              # update
              a[i]=x_best
              s += x_best - old
              C = CB + x_best * Lvec
              if di < L: C[di] += x_best*x_best
              # Clean tiny negatives?
              # C should nonnegative but due roundoff
              # cur_score = new_score (or recompute max(C)/s^2)
              # Ensure consistency: cur_score = 2*n*np.max(C)/(s*s)
              # But due C update maybe small; use new_score.
              cur_score = new_score
              improved_this_cycle +=1
              if cur_score < best_score:
                  best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
       if cycle % 5 == 0: print...
       if improved_this_cycle == 0 and cycle > 2: maybe perturb? Could break and outside restart.
       if cycle % recompute_every == 0:
          C = _fft_convolve(a); C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
          a_sum=a.sum(); if a_sum>0: a/=a_sum; C/=a_sum^2; s=1
          cur_score=_score_fast(a)
          if cur_score < best_score: best...
       if improved_this_cycle == 0:
          # maybe try random restart perturbation? Could add noise to a and see if better?
          perturbed = a.copy()
          # multiply by (1+0.05*randn), clip, normalize
          noise = 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n) * (a + 1/n)
          perturbed = np.maximum(a+noise,0)
          pert_score=_score_fast(perturbed)
          if pert_score < cur_score * 1.02? Actually if worse, coordinate from perturbed might find new basin; but we only want best. We can allow up to e.g. cur_score+0.01 to escape, but risk time. Maybe do limited.
```

Let's optimize `_fft_convolve`:
```
def _fft_convolve(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=2*n-1
    m=1
    while m < size: m <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m)
    return c[:size]
```
Could use `scipy.fft`? numpy fine.

Direct evaluate_sequence uses np.convolve, which for large n maybe uses direct O(n^2). If final returns n=5000, one call maybe 25M multiplications, okay maybe <1s? 10 calls maybe 10s. Keep n<=2000 to be safe.

Candidate generation profiles:
```
def _profile(n, kind, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if kind=='uniform': a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind=='lin_inc': a=t
    elif kind=='lin_dec': a=1-t
    elif kind=='quad_inc': a=t*t
    elif kind=='quad_sym': a=(t-0.5)**2+0.01? maybe.
    elif kind=='cubic': a=t**3
    elif kind=='beta_pb': a=t**p*(1-t)**q? We can parameter by kind string? Use tuple? Simpler in propose loop generate via `_beta_profile(n,p,q)`.
    elif kind=='gauss': a=np.exp(-((t-c)**2)/(2*w^2))
    elif kind=='periodic': a=base + amp*np.sin(2π f t+phase), clip
    random smooth: rng normal convolved with small kernel.
```
Need top-level functions no closures; can pass parameters.

def _blend(a1,a2, alpha):
    if lengths same: b = (1-alpha)*np.log(a1+eps)+alpha*np.log(a2+eps); b=exp(b); normalize

Now, coordinate candidate grid construction needs to be efficient and top-level. We can precompute coarse u per n? In `_coord_descent`, compute once.
```
def _make_u_grid(n):
    u0=1.0/n
    lo=max(1e-7, u0*1e-6)
    hi=5.0
    log=np.logspace(np.log10(lo), np.log10(hi), 80)
    lin=np.linspace(0.0, max(0.05, 20*u0), 80)
    u=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], log, lin)))
    # sort
    return u
```
If n=50, u0=0.02, lin up to0.5? max(0.05,0.4)=0.4. If n=2000, u0=.0005, lin up to0.01? max(0.05,0.01)=0.05; maybe enough, log covers to5. But optimum likely ~1/n; log covers.

Candidate evaluation helper:
```
def _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X):
    if X.size == 0: return np.array([np.inf])
    # For memory, if X.size * CB.size > 2_000_000, chunk
    maxc = np.empty(X.size)
    chunk = max(1, 2000000 // max(1, CB.size))
    for start in range(0, X.size, chunk):
       Xe=X[start:start+chunk]
       vals=CB[None,:] + Xe[:,None]*Lvec[None,:]
       if di >=0:
          self_=CB[di] + Xe*Xe
          maxc_chunk=np.maximum(np.max(vals, axis=1), self_)
       else:
          maxc_chunk=np.max(vals, axis=1)
       maxc[start:start+chunk]=maxc_chunk/(sB+Xe)**2
    return maxc
```
No lambda. Use `di` int or -1. This chunk uses 2D for up to 2M elements (16MB), okay. For n=500, chunk=2000 (M~80 all at once). For n=3000, L5999, chunk=333.

```
def _sort_candidates(cands):
    # cands list of [score, arr]; insertion sort by score
    out=[]
    for c in cands:
       i=len(out)
       while i>0 and out[i-1][0] > c[0]:
           out[i]=out[i-1]; i-=1
       out[i]=c
    return out
```

Let's implement `_normalize(a)`:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    # If sum too small, add small? If all zeros, uniform
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12:
       a=np.ones_like(a)/max(1,a.size)
    else:
       a=a/s
    return a
```
Scale invariant; final sum1 >0.01.

`_score` should handle length maybe 0. Use `_fft_convolve`; if n small, max positive. If sum <0.01 return inf (but normalized not). Need clip? Input candidates normalized nonnegative.
```
def _score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return inf
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 0.01: return inf
   c=_fft_convolve(a)
   maxc=float(np.max(c))
   if not np.isfinite(maxc): return inf
   return float(2.0*n*maxc/(s*s))
```
`_fft_convolve` for n=1: size1, m=1, A rfft length1, c [a0^2]. good.

Now coordinate function detailed implementation:
```
def _coord_descent(a_init, deadline, max_cycles=200, seed=0, verbose=True):
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    C=_fft_convolve(a); C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
    s=1.0
    cur_score=_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_score): return a, cur_score
    best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    u_grid=_make_u_grid(n)
    # optionally if n>2000, reduce points
    if n > 1500: u_grid = u_grid[::2]
    print? use verbose to limit output.
    for cyc in range(max_cycles):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        order=np.arange(n); rng.shuffle(order)
        improved=0
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            old=a[i]
            sB=s-old
            if sB <= 1e-14:
                continue
            Lvec=np.zeros(L)
            Lvec[i:i+n] = 2.0 * a
            di=2*i
            if di < L:
                Lvec[di] = 0.0
            CB=C - old*Lvec
            if di < L:
                CB[di] -= old*old
            CB=np.maximum(CB,0.0)
            # candidate X
            X = u_grid * sB
            # add old
            X = np.concatenate((X, np.array([old])))
            # maybe if old/sB not in grid and old large; unique? skip
            mvals = _coord_eval(CB,Lvec,di,sB,X)
            idx=int(np.argmin(mvals))
            x_best = float(X[idx]); m_best=float(mvals[idx])
            # refine
            x_best,m_best = _coord_refine(CB,Lvec,di,sB,x_best,m_best,old,n)
            new_score=float(2*n*m_best)
            if new_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
                a[i]=x_best
                s += x_best - old
                C = CB + x_best*Lvec
                if di < L: C[di] += x_best*x_best
                # optionally clip C negative
                cur_score = new_score
                improved +=1
                if cur_score < best:
                    best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
        if cyc % 5 ==0:
            if verbose: print(f"   coord n={n} cyc={cyc} score={cur_score:.6f} best={best:.6f} time={time.monotonic()-start?}")
        if cyc >0 and improved==0:
            # try larger grid? no. break
            break
        if cyc % 4 ==3:
            # recompute to correct
            C=_fft_convolve(a); C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
            ss=a.sum()
            if ss>0:
                a=a/ss; C=C/(ss*ss); s=1.0
            cur_score=_score(a)
            if cur_score < best:
                best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
    # final recompute and normalize
    ss=a.sum(); if ss>0: a=a/ss
    C=_fft_convolve(a); C=np.maximum(C,0)
    cur_score=_score(a)
    if cur_score < best: best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a, best
```
Need `_coord_refine` top-level.

def _coord_refine(CB,Lvec,di,sB,x_best,m_best,old,n):
   if sB <=0: return x_best,m_best
   if x_best > 1e-10:
       # first medium
       lo = max(0.0, x_best*0.3)
       hi = x_best*3.0
       if hi <= lo: hi = lo + sB*1e-4
       u_lo=lo/sB; u_hi=hi/sB
       # If u_hi/u_lo too small? 
       Xf = np.linspace(lo, hi, 60)
       if u_lo > 0 and u_hi > u_lo:
           Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, sB*np.logspace(np.log10(u_lo), np.log10(u_hi), 40)))
       Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, [x_best, old]))
       mf=_coord_eval(CB,Lvec,di,sB,Xf)
       idx=np.argmin(mf)
       x1=float(Xf[idx]); m1=float(mf[idx])
       if m1 < m_best: x_best,m_best=x1,m1
       # ultra
       if x_best > 1e-12:
           lo2=max(0.0,x_best*0.85); hi2=x_best*1.15
           if hi2>lo2:
              zu=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,40)
              z=np.concatenate((zu,[x_best,old]))
              mz=_coord_eval(...)
              idx...
   else:
       # check small positive
       xmax = max(1.0/n*20, 1e-5)
       Xf = sB*np.linspace(0.0, xmax, 40)
       Xf = np.concatenate((Xf,[old]))
       mf=...
   return x_best,m_best

`_coord_eval` chunk. Need handle `di` maybe -1; but di always valid because 2i <=2n-2 = L-1. So always valid. We can use di int. For all coordinates, self exists. Thus no -1. Good. In `_coord_eval`, self always.
```
def _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X):
    X=np.asarray(X,float)
    out=np.empty(X.size)
    Llen=CB.size
    if X.size==0: return out
    max_elem=2000000
    chunk=max(1, max_elem//max(1,Llen))
    for st in range(0,X.size,chunk):
       Xe=X[st:st+chunk]
       # if Xe size 1, broadcasting okay
       vals=CB[None,:] + Xe[:,None]*Lvec[None,:]
       selfv=CB[di] + Xe*Xe
       maxv=np.maximum(np.max(vals, axis=1), selfv)
       denom=(sB+Xe)**2
       # denom positive because sB>0 or Xe>0; if zero set inf
       denom[denom<=0]=np.inf
       out[st:st+chunk]=maxv/denom
    return out
```
When Xe empty? no. `vals` memory 2M. If Xe size1, `vals` (1,L), fine.
This recomputes CB+... each chunk; could be heavy but okay.

Potential memory with n=5000 L9999, chunk=200; X~100 -> one chunk 2M (16MB) okay.

Candidate grid `_make_u_grid` no lambda:
```
def _make_u_grid(n, coarse_points=120):
    u0=1.0/n
    lo=max(1e-8, u0*1e-5)
    hi=5.0
    n1=coarse_points//2
    loggrid=np.logspace(np.log10(lo), np.log10(hi), n1)
    linhi=max(0.05, 20*u0)
    if linhi > 1.0: linhi=1.0
    lingrid=np.linspace(0.0, linhi, coarse_points-n1)
    u=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0],loggrid,lingrid)))
    return u
```

Subgradient function:
```
def _subgradient_smooth(a_init, deadline, max_iters=200000, alpha=0.5, peaks_power=10.0, top_k=32, verbose=True):
   a=_normalize(a_init); n; L
   C=_fft_convolve(a); score
   best...
   lr=alpha/n
   decay=0.99995
   stalls=0
   start=time.monotonic()
   while time<deadline:
      C=_fft_convolve(a); maxC=C.max(); if maxC<=0 break
      score=2n*maxC
      if score<best: best...
      # active weights
      if top_k>=L: idx=np.arange(L)
      else:
         if top_k<1: top_k=1
         idx=np.argpartition(C, -top_k)[-top_k:]
      cidx=C[idx]
      # weights by power
      w=(cidx/maxC)**peaks_power
      wsum=w.sum()
      if wsum<=0: break
      w=w/wsum
      S=np.zeros(n)
      # loop over idx,w
      for kk in range(len(idx)):
          k=int(idx[kk]); wk=float(w[kk])
          if wk <= 1e-7: continue
          start=max(0,k-n+1); end=min(n,k+1)
          if start<end:
              js=np.arange(start,end)
              S[js] += wk * 2.0 * a[k-js]
      smax=S.max()
      if smax <= 1e-15: break
      S=S/smax
      # backtrack
      accepted=False
      for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          trial=a - lr*factor*S
          trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0)
          ss=trial.sum()
          if ss<=0: continue
          trial=trial/ss
          tsc=_score(trial)
          if tsc < cur_score - 1e-12:
              a=trial; cur_score=tsc; accepted=True; break
          if tsc < best + 1e-6 and tsc < cur_score + 1e-8:
              # accept small? maybe not
              pass
      if accepted:
          if cur_score < best: best=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
          lr *= decay
          stalls=0
      else:
          stalls +=1
          lr *= 0.5
          if lr < 1e-14 or stalls > 100: break
      if verbose and it%5000==0: print
   return best_a,best
```

```

Geometric blend top two candidates:
```
def _blend_exp(a,b,alpha):
   # same length
   eps=1e-12
   y=(1-alpha)*np.log(a+eps)+alpha*np.log(b+eps)
   y=y - y.max()
   c=np.exp(y)
   return normalize(c)
```

    # Add parent if size 20..2500

       if parent.size <= 2500: add parent

Implement `_lp_alternate` carefully top-level:
```
def _lp_alternate(a_init, deadline, max_iters=40, initial_blend=0.7):
    if not _HAS_LINPROG: return _normalize(a_init), _score(_normalize(a_init))
    a=_normalize(a_init); n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    if n>400: a=_interpolate_sequence(a,400); n=400; L=799
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a); blend=initial_blend
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       # M should be max f*f? use full conv max
       c=_fft_convolve(a); M=float(np.max(c))
       if M<=0: break
       # Build C_mat (L x n): (C @ g)[k] = sum_j g_j f_{k-j}? Since convolution f*g coefficient k = ∑_{i+j=k} f_i g_j. For fixed f, matrix row k, col j: f_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n else 0.
       Cmat=np.zeros((L,n))
       for j in range(n): # complex vector O(n^2); n 300 ~90k ok
           start=max(0,j-n+1)? Wait col j: contributions to k where f_i g_j with i+j=k => k=i+j, i=0..n-1. So column j is f shifted by j: Cmat[j:j+n, j] = f.
           Cmat[j:j+n, j] = f
       # But if j+j? For i? This matches convolution row k col j=f[k-j]. yes.
       c_obj=-np.ones(n); b_ub=M*np.ones(L); bounds=[(0,None)]*n
       try: res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=Cmat,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
       except: return best,best_score
       if res.status!=0 or res.x is None: return best,best_score
       g=res.x; sumg=float(np.sum(g)); if sumg<1e-12: break
       g/=sumg
       new=(1-blend)*a + blend*g; new=np.maximum(new,0); snew=np.sum(new); if snew<=0 break
       new/=snew
       sc=_score(new)
       if sc < best_score:
           best_score=sc; best=new.copy()
       a=new; blend=max(0.2, blend*0.97)
    return best,best_score
```
Check Cmat column: f*g coefficient k = ∑_i f_i g_{k-i}. For fixed j (g_j), k=i+j => row k from i=0..n-1. So rows j..j+n-1 column j = f[0..n-1]. Yes. Matrix size (2n-1)x n. Good.
LP objective max sum g. The previous used this but M max f*f. Good.

But for a sum1, M ~1/n. For uniform n=300 M=1/300=0.0033. LP constraints f*g <=0.0033. If g has sum1, f*g coefficients? Maybe feasible? It will find some small g. Could improve.
Time: linprog high with 300 vars, 599 constraints, 40 iters. Maybe 0.1s each? 4s. good. Add for top 3 n<=400 for 60s each.

Detailed code for `_add_candidate`:
```
def _add_candidate(records, a, max_records=60):
    if a is None: return records
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return records
    if arr.size > 2500:
        arr=_interpolate_sequence(arr,2000)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-9:
        arr=np.ones_like(arr)
        s=float(arr.sum())
    if s > 1.0:
        arr=arr/s  # normalize sum1 for scoring
        s=1.0
    else:
        arr=arr/s
    sc=_score(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return records
    # remove if duplicate exact
    for rec in records:
        if rec[1].shape == arr.shape and np.max(np.abs(rec[1]-arr)) < 1e-10:
            # keep lower score
            return records
    records.append([sc, arr.copy()])
    if len(records) > max_records:
        # find worst
        worst_idx=0; worst_sc=records[0][0]
        for idx in range(1,len(records)):
            if records[idx][0] > worst_sc:
                worst_sc=records[idx][0]; worst_idx=idx
        records.pop(worst_idx)
    return records
```
If new worse than worst, still pop worst (new remains). Good. If duplicate with worse new, ignore. Duplicate check O(60*n) each add, 600*60*1000 ~36M comparisons, maybe okay but could slow. We can skip duplicate check or use simple length and score? To save, skip exact duplicate. Duplicates not harmful. Or only check if same length and score close? Not needed. Remove duplicate check for speed. Duplicates coordinate redundant but top selection may include duplicates. Could cause coordinate multiple same. We can later select diverse? Maybe simple: in final top selection, avoid duplicates by comparing to selected. But not critical. Let's remove expensive duplicate check. Instead in `_top_candidates` after sorting, build selected avoiding arrays with max abs diff <1e-6 from already selected if same length. O(12*60*n) small.

`_sort_candidates`:
```
def _sort_candidates(cands):
    out=[]
    for c in cands:
        i=len(out)
        while i>0 and out[i-1][0] > c[0]:
            out[i]=out[i-1]
            i-=1
        out[i]=c
    return out
```
If cands list large, O(n^2) 3600, fine.

for c in _sort_candidates(cands):
   duplicate=False
   for d in out:
      if d[1].shape==c[1].shape:
         # If score too close and diff small? use average abs
         diff=np.mean(np.abs(d[1]-c[1]))
         if diff < tol:
            duplicate=True; break
   if not duplicate:
      out.append(c)
      if len(out)==k: break
return out
```

Profile functions:
```
def _profile_power(n,alpha):
    if n<=1: return np.ones(max(n,1))
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-12:
        a=np.ones(n)
    else:
        a=t**alpha
    return _normalize(a)
```
If alpha large, tail. Reversal separate.

`_profile_beta`:
```
def _profile_beta(n,p,q):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if p==0: left=np.ones(n)
    else: left=t**p
    if q==0: right=np.ones(n)
    else: right=(1.0-t)**q
    a=left*right
    return _normalize(a)
```
Zeros endpoints if p/q >0; ok.

`_profile_sine`:
```
def _profile_sine(n, amp1, f1, ph1, amp2, f2, ph2):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    a=1.0 + amp1*np.cos(2*np.pi*f1*t + ph1) + amp2*np.cos(2*np.pi*f2*t + ph2)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    return _normalize(a)
```
Maybe add small floor? If all zero, normalize uniform.

`_profile_random`:
```
def _profile_random(n,rng):
    r=np.abs(rng.normal(size=n)) + 0.05
    w=max(1,n//50)
    if w>1:
       # moving average via cumsum
       c=np.cumsum(r)
       a=(c[w-1:] - np.concatenate(([0.0], c[:-w])))/w
       # edges
       a[0]=r[0]
       a[-1]=r[-1]
       # better simple convolution direct
    else: a=r
   # maybe apply small exponential smoothing? simple convolve
    k=np.ones(w)/w
    a=np.convolve(r,k,mode='same')
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    return _normalize(a)
```
`np.convolve` direct O(n*w); okay. Use for w maybe n//20, for n=2000 w100 ->200k. fine.

`_perturb`:
```
def _perturb(a, rng, rel):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a)
   noise = rel * (a + 1.0/n) * rng.normal(size=n)
   b = np.maximum(a + noise, 0.0)
   return _normalize(b)
```

`_blend_exp`:
```
def _blend_exp(a,b,alpha):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.asarray(b,float)
   if a.shape != b.shape: b=_interpolate_sequence(b,a.size)
   eps=1e-12
   y=(1-alpha)*np.log(a+eps) + alpha*np.log(b+eps)
   y = y - np.max(y)
   c=np.exp(y)
   return _normalize(c)
```

Subgradient: Use `_score` each trial. Could be heavy. Add `max_iters` and break. Use top_k maybe 16 for n large.
Implementation:
```
def _subgradient_smooth(a_init, deadline, max_iters=50000, alpha=1.0, peaks_power=8.0, top_k=24, verbose=False):
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    cur=_score(a); best=cur; best_a=a.copy()
    lr=alpha/n
    stalls=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if it>max_iters: break
       c=_fft_convolve(a); maxc=float(np.max(c))
       if maxc <= 0: break
       cur=2.0*n*maxc  # since a sum1? ensure normalize maintained
       if cur < best: best=cur; best_a=a.copy()
       if top_k >= L:
           idx=np.arange(L)
       else:
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       vals=c[idx] / maxc
       w=vals ** peaks_power
       sw=w.sum()
       if sw <=0: break
       w=w/sw
       S=np.zeros(n)
       for t in range(len(idx)):
           k=int(idx[t]); wk=float(w[t])
           if wk < 1e-8: continue
           st=max(0,k-n+1); en=min(n,k+1)
           if st < en:
               js=np.arange(st,en)
               S[js] += wk * 2.0 * a[k-js]
       smax=float(np.max(S))
       if smax <= 1e-15: break
       S=S/smax
       accepted=False
       for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
           trial=a - lr*factor*S
           trial=np.maximum(trial,0.0)
           ss=float(trial.sum())
           if ss <= 1e-12: continue
           trial=trial/ss
           tsc=_score(trial)
           if tsc < cur - 1e-13:
               a=trial; cur=tsc; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           stalls=0
           lr *= 0.9999
           if lr < 1e-8/n: lr=1e-8/n
       else:
           stalls +=1
           lr *= 0.5
           if lr < 1e-10/n or stalls > 200:
               break
       if verbose and it % 2000 ==0:
           print(f"      subgrad iter {it} best={best:.6f} cur={cur:.6f}")
    # final normalize
    a=_normalize(a)
    sc=_score(a)
    if sc<best: best=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best
```
Using c from FFT but cur computed as 2n*maxc only if a sum1. We normalize trial to sum1; a maintained sum1. Good. If due roundoff, ok.

Could `_score(trial)` recompute conv, expensive. But top_k small. For n=1000, each `_score` 4096 FFT; 3 trials per iter, 50k iters too much. Limit subgradient iterations for large n: `max_iters = 20000 if n<1000 else 5000`. Or time-based. Use time. For n=1000, 5000*3 FFT ~15000 FFT length2048, maybe seconds 10-30. okay. For n=2000, 2000 iters. Add `max_iters = max(500, min(50000, 2000000//n))` maybe. We'll set in call.

Coordinate `_score` not called per coordinate, uses vector scores. Good.

Now, coordinate C update: `C = CB + x_best * Lvec` alloc. If `CB` already clipped. After update, maybe `C=np.maximum(C,0.0)`? Could cost; do every cycle recompute. Maybe after each update not clip; if small negatives, candidate max maybe unaffected (max with self nonnegative). But self maybe CB[di]+x^2 nonnegative. Other negative could be max if all others negative? no. Keep no clip for speed. At recompute clip.

In `_coord_descent`, before each coordinate, C may have small negative; CB negative? Could maximize negative? Max likely positive. But for safety, when evaluating, we can clip CB? We do `CB=np.maximum(CB,0.0)`. This cost small. C itself maybe small negatives at inactive; not harm. C update could accumulate; recompute every 4 cycles.

```
def propose_candidate():
    import? no, already imports.
    start=time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline=start+2320.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(20240531)

       parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=np.float64).ravel()

        _add_candidate(records,a_lp)
        lp_done+=1
        print(...)
    # Sort and select diverse
    selected=_select_diverse(records, 14)
    # Coordinate main
    coord_end=min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+900.0)
    # assign allocation based on score: first few more
    for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(selected):
        if time.monotonic()>coord_end: break
        n=len(a)
        if n>1200: continue? Maybe if n>1200 skip coordinate, but selected likely small. If skip and all large, subgrad.
        # allocate: if idx<4: 120s, else 60s, but not exceed
        alloc = 120 if idx<4 else 60
        cd = min(coord_end, time.monotonic()+alloc)
        print(f"Coord on candidate score {sc:.6f} n={n}")
        a_opt, sc_opt = _coord_descent(a, cd, max_cycles=5000, seed=100+idx, verbose=(idx==0))
        _add_candidate(records,a_opt)
        if sc_opt < sc: print(...)
    # After coordinate, best record maybe top.
    best_a, best_sc = _top_one(records)
    # Subgradient phase
    sub_end=min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+400.0)
    # run on top 3 diverse that are not coordinate? just top
    sub_cands=_select_diverse(records, 5)
    for idx,(sc,a) in enumerate(sub_cands):
       if time.monotonic()>sub_end: break
       n=len(a)
       alloc=150 if idx<2 else 80
       dl=min(sub_end, time.monotonic()+alloc)
       a_opt, sc_opt = _subgradient_smooth(a, dl, max_iters=20000 if n<=1000 else 5000, alpha=1.0, peaks_power=8, top_k=24 if n<=1000 else 16, verbose=(idx==0))
       _add_candidate(records,a_opt)
       # quick coordinate polish on subgrad result if time
       if time.monotonic() < sub_end - 20:
           a_pol, sc_pol = _coord_descent(a_opt, min(sub_end, time.monotonic()+30.0), max_cycles=200, seed=900+idx, verbose=False)
           _add_candidate(records,a_pol)
    # Final polish: best record
    best_a,best_sc=_top_one(records)
    polish_end=hard_deadline-60.0
    if best_a.size<=1500 and time.monotonic() < polish_end:
       # coordinate with remaining
       a_opt,sc_opt=_coord_descent(best_a, polish_end, max_cycles=10000, seed=777, verbose=False)
       _add_candidate(records,a_opt)
       best_a,best_sc=_top_one(records)
       # subgrad with remaining maybe 120s
       if time.monotonic() < polish_end-1:
          a_opt,sc_opt=_subgradient_smooth(best_a, min(polish_end, time.monotonic()+120.0), max_iters=10000, verbose=False)
          _add_candidate(records,a_opt)
          best_a,best_sc=_top_one(records)
    # perturb restarts if time left before final reserve
    restart_end=time.monotonic()
    # Actually use remaining to polish top.
    # Build final candidates: top diverse 12 plus maybe raw parent
    final_cands=_select_diverse(records, 12)
    # Also ensure parent/downsampled fallback uniform
    fallback=np.ones(100)
    best_val=np.inf; best_seq=fallback
    for sc,a in final_cands:
       seq=_prepare_final(a)
       try:
          val=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       except Exception:
          val=sc
       if val < best_val:
          best_val=val; best_seq=seq
    # If no finite, fallback
    if not np.isfinite(best_val):
       best_seq=[1.0]*100
    # Optionally if best_val > 1.6, try direct linear? But linear may >2; uniform 2. Still.
    return list(best_seq)
```

```
def _select_diverse(cands,k,tol=1e-6):
    srt=_sort_candidates(cands)
    out=[]
    for item in srt:
       dup=False
       for other in out:
          if other[1].shape == item[1].shape:
             # compare mean abs
             diff=float(np.mean(np.abs(other[1]-item[1])))
             if diff < tol:
                dup=True; break
       if not dup:
          out.append(item)
          if len(out)==k: break
    return out
```

Detailed code snippets:

```
def _fft_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2*n - 1
    m = 1
    while m < size:
        m <<= 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return c[:size].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```
`astype` okay. If a contains nan? candidates finite.

`_score`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01: return inf
    c=_fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return inf
    return float(2.0*n*mx/(s*s))
```
If mx negative due all negative? candidates nonneg. If mx<=0, score0? Could be inf? Not. Return 0 possible if all zeros but sum<0.01. fine.

`_interpolate`:
```
def _interpolate_sequence(src,n):
    src=np.asarray(src,float).ravel()
    if src.size==0: return np.ones(n)
    if src.size==n: return src.copy().astype(float,copy=True)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,src.size)
    y=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(y,x,src)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s>1e-12: out=out/s
    else: out=np.ones(n)/n
    return out
```
Returning normalized? `_add_candidate` normalizes anyway. But for coordinate initial, normalized. Good. However if we interpolate to get a profile, scores independent of scale. Normalize okay.

```
def _make_u_grid(n, points=140):
    u0=1.0/max(1,n)
    lo=max(1e-8, 1e-5*u0)
    hi=5.0
    nlog=points//2
    nl=points-nlog
    loggrid=np.logspace(np.log10(lo), np.log10(hi), nlog)
    linhi=max(0.05, 20*u0)
    if linhi > 1.0: linhi=1.0
    lingrid=np.linspace(0.0, linhi, nl)
    u=np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], loggrid, lingrid)))
    return u
```
If points small? default 140; nlog70. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative height sequence minimizing
J(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The solver combines:
- many smooth, power-law, sinusoidal, periodic and random initial profiles;
- an exact-style one-coordinate search using the explicit formula for the
  effect of changing one coefficient in a polynomial square;
- active-set subgradient smoothing;
- the previous LP alternating iteration as a fallback/explorer;
- final verification with the provided evaluate_sequence().
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAS_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _HAS_LINPROG = False


def _fft_convolve(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    m = 1
    while m < size:
        m <<= 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return c[:size].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _score(a):
    """Our fast float score, matching evaluate_sequence except direct conv."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _normalize(a):
    """Clip to [0,1000] and scale to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _interpolate_sequence(src, n):
    """Interpolate to length n and normalize."""
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if src.size == 0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return out / float(n)
    if src.size == n:
        out = src.copy()
    else:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size)
        xt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(xt, xs, src)
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s > 1e-12:
        out = out / s
    else:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out


def _add_candidate(records, a, max_records=64):
    """Add a candidate if finite, normalized, and not too large."""
    if a is None:
        return records
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return records
    if arr.size > 2500:
        arr = _interpolate_sequence(arr, 2000)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(arr))
    if s > 1e-12:
        arr = arr / s
    else:
        arr = np.ones_like(arr, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    sc = _score(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return records
    records.append([sc, arr.copy()])
    if len(records) > max_records:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_sc = records[0][0]
        for j in range(1, len(records)):
            if records[j][0] > worst_sc:
                worst_sc = records[j][0]
                worst_idx = j
        records.pop(worst_idx)
    return records


def _sort_candidates(cands):
    """Insertion-sort by score ascending; no lambdas."""
    out = []
    for item in cands:
        i = len(out)
        while i > 0 and out[i - 1][0] > item[0]:
            out[i] = out[i - 1]
            i -= 1
        out[i] = item
    return out


def _select_diverse(cands, k, tol=1e-7):
    """Return up to k diversely different candidates."""
    srt = _sort_candidates(cands)
    out = []
    for item in srt:
        dup = False
        for other in out:
            if other[1].shape == item[1].shape:
                diff = float(np.mean(np.abs(other[1] - item[1])))
                if diff < tol:
                    dup = True
                    break
        if not dup:
            out.append(item)
            if len(out) == k:
                break
    return out


def _top_one(cands):
    if not cands:
        return np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64), float("inf")
    srt = _sort_candidates(cands)
    return srt[0][1].copy(), srt[0][0]


def _profile_power(n, alpha):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = t ** alpha
    return _normalize(a)


def _profile_beta(n, p, q):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    left = np.ones_like(t) if abs(p) < 1e-12 else t ** p
    right = np.ones_like(t) if abs(q) < 1e-12 else (1.0 - t) ** q
    a = left * right
    return _normalize(a)


def _profile_sine(n, amp1, f1, ph1, amp2, f2, ph2):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = (
        1.0
        + amp1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * t + ph1)
        + amp2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * t + ph2)
    )
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _profile_periodic(n, period, amp, rng):
    if period <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    base = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(period) / float(period) + ph)
    base = np.maximum(base, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(base)) <= 1e-15:
        base = np.ones(period, dtype=np.float64)
    reps = n // period + 1
    a = np.tile(base, reps)[:n]
    return _normalize(a)


def _profile_random(n, rng):
    r = np.abs(rng.normal(size=n)) + 0.05
    w = max(1, int(n // 50))
    if w > 1:
        a = np.convolve(r, np.ones(w) / float(w), mode="same")
    else:
        a = r
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    return _normalize(a)


def _perturb(a, rng, rel):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    noise = rel * (a + 1.0 / float(n)) * rng.normal(size=n)
    b = np.maximum(a + noise, 0.0)
    return _normalize(b)


def _blend_exp(a, b, alpha):
    """Geometric blend; both non-negative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size == 0:
        b = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.shape != b.shape:
        b = _interpolate_sequence(b, a.size)
    eps = 1e-12
    y = (1.0 - alpha) * np.log(a + eps) + alpha * np.log(b + eps)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    c = np.exp(y)
    return _normalize(c)


def _generate_candidates(records, parent, rng, n_list, deadline):
    for n in n_list:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            return

        # Smooth/power shapes.
        for alpha in [0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0]:
            a = _profile_power(n, alpha)
            _add_candidate(records, a)
            _add_candidate(records, a[::-1])

        # Beta shapes.
        for p in [0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
            for q in [0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
                if abs(p) < 1e-9 and abs(q) < 1e-9:
                    continue
                a = _profile_beta(n, p, q)
                _add_candidate(records, a)
                _add_candidate(records, a[::-1])

        # Low-frequency sinusoids.
        for amp1 in [0.2, 0.5, 0.8]:
            for f1 in [1, 2, 3, 5]:
                ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                a = _profile_sine(n, amp1, float(f1), ph1, 0.5 * amp1, float(2 * f1 + 1), ph2)
                _add_candidate(records, a)
                _add_candidate(records, a[::-1])

        # Periodic high-frequency shapes.
        for period in [2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 11]:
            for amp in [0.2, 0.6, 1.0]:
                a = _profile_periodic(n, period, amp, rng)
                _add_candidate(records, a)
                _add_candidate(records, a[::-1])

        # Random smoothed shapes.
        for _ in range(4):
            a = _profile_random(n, rng)
            _add_candidate(records, a)

        # Parent-derived shapes.
        if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 and float(np.sum(parent)) > 0:
            a = _interpolate_sequence(parent, n)
            _add_candidate(records, a)
            _add_candidate(records, a[::-1])
            _add_candidate(records, _perturb(a, rng, 0.05))
            _add_candidate(records, _perturb(a, rng, 0.12))

    # Parent itself, if manageable.
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size <= 2500:
            _add_candidate(records, parent)
            _add_candidate(records, parent[::-1])
        else:
            _add_candidate(records, _interpolate_sequence(parent, 1500))


def _make_u_grid(n, points=140):
    """Candidate x / sB grid for one-coordinate search."""
    u0 = 1.0 / float(max(1, n))
    lo = max(1e-8, 1e-5 * u0)
    hi = 5.0
    nlog = max(10, points // 2)
    nlin = max(10, points - nlog)
    logg = np.logspace(np.log10(lo), np.log10(hi), nlog)
    linhi = max(0.05, 20.0 * u0)
    if linhi > 1.0:
        linhi = 1.0
    ling = np.linspace(0.0, linhi, nlin)
    u = np.unique(np.concatenate(([0.0], logg, ling)))
    return u


def _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X):
    """
    Evaluate m(x)=max_k(CB_k + L_k x + delta_k x^2)/(sB+x)^2 for all x in X.
    delta is 1 at k=di, zero otherwise.
    """
    X = np.asarray(X, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.empty(X.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if X.size == 0:
        return out
    Llen = CB.size
    max_elem = 1_200_000
    chunk = max(1, max_elem // max(1, Llen))
    for st in range(0, X.size, chunk):
        Xe = X[st:st + chunk]
        vals = CB[None, :] + Xe[:, None] * Lvec[None, :]
        selfv = CB[di] + Xe * Xe
        maxv = np.maximum(np.max(vals, axis=1), selfv)
        denom = (sB + Xe) ** 2
        bad = denom <= 1e-300
        if np.any(bad):
            maxv = np.where(bad, np.inf, maxv)
            denom = np.where(bad, 1.0, denom)
        out[st:st + chunk] = maxv / denom
    return out


def _coord_refine(CB, Lvec, di, sB, x_best, m_best, old, n):
    """Local refinement around the best one-coordinate candidate."""
    if sB <= 1e-14:
        return float(x_best), float(m_best)

    x_best = float(x_best)
    m_best = float(m_best)

    if x_best > 1e-10:
        lo = max(0.0, 0.25 * x_best)
        hi = max(lo + 1e-12, 3.0 * x_best)
        Xf = np.linspace(lo, hi, 60)
        if lo > 0.0:
            Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, np.logspace(np.log10(lo), np.log10(hi), 40)))
        Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, [x_best, max(0.0, float(old))]))
        mf = _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, Xf)
        idx = int(np.argmin(mf))
        if mf[idx] < m_best:
            x_best = float(Xf[idx])
            m_best = float(mf[idx])

        if x_best > 1e-12:
            lo2 = max(0.0, 0.85 * x_best)
            hi2 = 1.15 * x_best
            if hi2 > lo2:
                X2 = np.linspace(lo2, hi2, 40)
                X2 = np.concatenate((X2, [x_best, max(0.0, float(old))]))
                m2 = _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X2)
                idx2 = int(np.argmin(m2))
                if m2[idx2] < m_best:
                    x_best = float(X2[idx2])
                    m_best = float(m2[idx2])
    else:
        xmax_u = max(20.0 / float(max(1, n)), 1e-5)
        Xf = sB * np.linspace(0.0, xmax_u, 40)
        Xf = np.concatenate((Xf, [max(0.0, float(old))]))
        mf = _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, Xf)
        idx = int(np.argmin(mf))
        if mf[idx] < m_best:
            x_best = float(Xf[idx])
            m_best = float(mf[idx])

    return x_best, m_best


def _coord_descent(a_init, deadline, max_cycles=8000, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    One-coordinate descent.

    Maintains C = a*a. For coordinate i with old value old and all others B,
    new convolution is:
        C_B + 2*x*B shifted by i + x^2 at 2*i.
    This is evaluated over a grid plus local refinement.
    """
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, _score(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    C = _fft_convolve(a)
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    s = 1.0
    cur = _score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(cur):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        C = _fft_convolve(a)
        cur = _score(a)

    best = cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    u_grid = _make_u_grid(n, 160)
    if n > 1200:
        step = max(1, u_grid.size // 100)
        u_grid = u_grid[::step]

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    improved_streak = 0

    for cyc in range(max_cycles):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        order = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        rng.shuffle(order)
        improved = 0

        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break

            old = float(a[i])
            sB = s - old
            if sB <= 1e-14:
                continue

            Lvec = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            Lvec[i:i + n] = 2.0 * a
            di = 2 * i
            if di < L:
                Lvec[di] = 0.0

            CB = C - old * Lvec
            if di < L:
                CB[di] -= old * old
            CB = np.maximum(CB, 0.0)

            X = sB * u_grid
            X = np.concatenate((X, [old]))
            mvals = _coord_eval(CB, Lvec, di, sB, X)
            idx = int(np.argmin(mvals))
            x_best = float(X[idx])
            m_best = float(mvals[idx])

            x_best, m_best = _coord_refine(CB, Lvec, di, sB, x_best, m_best, old, n)
            new_score = float(2.0 * n * m_best)

            if new_score < cur - 1e-14:
                a[i] = x_best
                s += x_best - old
                C = CB + x_best * Lvec
                if di < L:
                    C[di] += x_best * x_best
                cur = new_score
                improved += 1
                if cur < best:
                    best = cur
                    best_a = a.copy()

        improved_streak = 0 if improved > 0 else improved_streak + 1
        if improved_streak >= 3:
            break

        if verbose and cyc % 25 == 0:
            print(f"      coord n={n} cycle={cyc} cur={cur:.7f} best={best:.7f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

        if cyc % 4 == 3:
            C = _fft_convolve(a)
            ss = float(np.sum(a))
            if ss > 1e-12:
                a = a / ss
                C = C / (ss * ss)
                s = 1.0
            else:
                a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
                C = _fft_convolve(a)
                s = 1.0
            C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
            cur = _score(a)
            if cur < best:
                best = cur
                best_a = a.copy()

    ss = float(np.sum(a))
    if ss > 1e-12:
        a = a / ss
    C = _fft_convolve(a)
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
    cur = _score(a)
    if cur < best:
        best = cur
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best


def _subgradient_smooth(a_init, deadline, max_iters=30000, alpha=1.0,
                        peaks_power=8.0, top_k=24, verbose=False):
    """
    Smoothed active-set subgradient on the simplex.
    Gradient of convolution coefficient k at coordinate j is 2*a[k-j] if valid.
    """
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return a, _score(a)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    cur = _score(a)
    best = cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    lr = alpha / float(n)
    stalls = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        C = _fft_convolve(a)
        C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
        mx = float(np.max(C))
        if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
            break
        cur = float(2.0 * n * mx)  # a is normalized to sum 1
        if cur < best:
            best = cur
            best_a = a.copy()

        if top_k >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
        else:
            top_k = max(1, int(min(top_k, L - 1 if L > 1 else 1)))
            idx = np.argpartition(C, -top_k)[-top_k:]

        vals = C[idx] / mx
        w = vals ** peaks_power
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if sw <= 1e-15:
            break
        w = w / sw

        S = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for t in range(len(idx)):
            k = int(idx[t])
            wk = float(w[t])
            if wk < 1e-9:
                continue
            st = max(0, k - n + 1)
            en = min(n, k + 1)
            if st < en:
                js = np.arange(st, en, dtype=np.int64)
                S[js] += wk * (2.0 * a[k - js])

        smax = float(np.max(S))
        if smax <= 1e-16:
            break
        S = S / smax

        accepted = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            trial = a - lr * factor * S
            trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
            ss = float(np.sum(trial))
            if ss <= 1e-12:
                continue
            trial = trial / ss
            tsc = _score(trial)
            if tsc < cur - 1e-13:
                a = trial
                cur = tsc
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            stalls = 0
            lr *= 0.9999
            if lr < 1e-9 / float(n):
                lr = 1e-9 / float(n)
        else:
            stalls += 1
            lr *= 0.5
            if lr < 1e-12 / float(n) or stalls > 250:
                break

        if verbose and it % 3000 == 0:
            print(f"      subgrad n={n} it={it} cur={cur:.7f} best={best:.7f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}s")

    ss = float(np.sum(a))
    if ss > 1e-12:
        a = a / ss
    sc = _score(a)
    if sc < best:
        best = sc
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best


def _lp_alternate(a_init, deadline, max_iters=35, initial_blend=0.7):
    """
    Previous LP alternating step:
    fix f, solve max sum(g) s.t. f*g <= max(f*f), g >= 0, then blend.
    """
    if not _HAS_LINPROG:
        a = _normalize(a_init)
        return a, _score(a)

    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = a.size
    if n > 420:
        a = _interpolate_sequence(a, 420)
        n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1

    best = _score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    blend = initial_blend

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        C = _fft_convolve(a)
        M = float(np.max(C))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        Cmat = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(n):
            Cmat[j:j + n, j] = a

        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = M * np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=Cmat, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            return best_a, best

        if getattr(res, "status", None) != 0:
            return best_a, best
        g = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if g is None:
            return best_a, best
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if g.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            return best_a, best
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if sg <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / sg
        new = (1.0 - blend) * a + blend * g
        new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)
        sn = float(np.sum(new))
        if sn <= 1e-12:
            break
        new = new / sn
        sc_new = _score(new)
        cur = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best:
            best = sc_new
            best_a = new.copy()
        # Allow some exploratory moves but avoid severe degradation.
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur + 0.08:
            a = new
            blend = max(0.25, blend * 0.96)

    return best_a, best


def _prepare_final(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 100
    if a.size > 2500:
        a = _interpolate_sequence(a, 2000)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-6:
        return [1.0] * 100
    a = a / s
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def propose_candidate():
    """
    Main search driver. Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t_start + 2300.0  # leave ~100s for final verification and return
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240531)

    records = []

    # Optional parent sequence from previous iteration.
    parent = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if parent.size > 0 and float(np.max(parent)) > 0:
            print(f"Parent sequence found: n={parent.size}, fast_score={_score(parent):.7f}")
        else:
            parent = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    _add_candidate(records, parent)

    # 1) Candidate generation.
    gen_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 1600.0, t_start + 80.0)
    n_list = [32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024]
    _generate_candidates(records, parent, rng, n_list, gen_deadline)
    if not records:
        records = [[2.0, np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64)]]

    # 2) LP exploration on a few moderate-size candidates.
    lp_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 1500.0, t_start + 220.0)
    if _HAS_LINPROG and time.monotonic() < lp_deadline:
        srt = _sort_candidates(records)
        lp_used = 0
        for sc, a in srt:
            if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline or lp_used >= 3:
                break
            n = a.size
            if n < 50 or n > 420:
                continue
            dl = min(lp_deadline, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
            a_lp, sc_lp = _lp_alternate(a, dl, max_iters=30, initial_blend=0.75)
            _add_candidate(records, a_lp)
            lp_used += 1
            print(f"LP candidate n={n}: {sc:.6f} -> {sc_lp:.6f}")

    # 3) Subgradient warm-up to create smoother basins.
    sub_deadline1 = min(hard_deadline - 1300.0, time.monotonic() + 220.0)
    top = _select_diverse(records, 5)
    for idx, (sc, a) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > sub_deadline1:
            break
        n = a.size
        if n > 2000:
            a = _interpolate_sequence(a, 1200)
            n = a.size
        alloc = 100.0 if idx < 2 else 60.0
        dl = min(sub_deadline1, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        max_it = 30000 if n <= 800 else 12000
        a_sub, sc_sub = _subgradient_smooth(
            a, dl,
            max_iters=max_it,
            alpha=1.0,
            peaks_power=8.0,
            top_k=24 if n <= 800 else 16,
            verbose=(idx == 0)
        )
        _add_candidate(records, a_sub)
        print(f"Subgrad warmup n={n}: {sc:.6f} -> {sc_sub:.6f}")

    # 4) Main coordinate descent.
    coord_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 700.0, time.monotonic() + 1000.0)
    sel = _select_diverse(records, 16)
    for idx, (sc, a) in enumerate(sel):
        if time.monotonic() > coord_deadline:
            break
        if a.size > 1100:
            a_try = _interpolate_sequence(a, 900)
        else:
            a_try = a.copy()
        alloc = 130.0 if idx < 4 else (80.0 if idx < 10 else 45.0)
        dl = min(coord_deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        a_opt, sc_opt = _coord_descent(
            a_try, dl,
            max_cycles=20000,
            seed=1000 + idx,
            verbose=(idx == 0)
        )
        _add_candidate(records, a_opt)
        # If operating on an interpolated shorter version, also keep the original-length point if it is better.
        if a_try.size != a.size:
            _add_candidate(records, a)
        print(f"Coord candidate {idx}: n={a_try.size} {sc:.6f} -> {sc_opt:.6f}")

    # 5) Second round: subgradient + quick coordinate polish.
    pol_deadline = min(hard_deadline - 240.0, time.monotonic() + 420.0)
    top = _select_diverse(records, 5)
    for idx, (sc, a) in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() > pol_deadline:
            break
        n = a.size
        if n > 1600:
            a = _interpolate_sequence(a, 1000)
            n = a.size
        alloc_sub = 110.0 if idx < 2 else 70.0
        dl_sub = min(pol_deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc_sub)
        max_it = 20000 if n <= 1000 else 8000
        a_sub, sc_sub = _subgradient_smooth(
            a, dl_sub,
            max_iters=max_it,
            alpha=1.2,
            peaks_power=10.0,
            top_k=30 if n <= 1000 else 18,
            verbose=False
        )
        _add_candidate(records, a_sub)

        dl_cd = min(pol_deadline, time.monotonic() + 50.0)
        a_cd, sc_cd = _coord_descent(a_sub, dl_cd, max_cycles=5000, seed=9000 + idx, verbose=False)
        _add_candidate(records, a_cd)
        print(f"Polish {idx}: n={n} subgrad={sc_sub:.6f} coord={sc_cd:.6f}")

    # 6) Final focused polish from the best record.
    best_a, best_sc = _top_one(records)
    final_coord_dl = hard_deadline - 80.0
    if best_a.size > 0 and time.monotonic() < final_coord_dl - 10:
        if best_a.size > 1400:
            a_to_polish = _interpolate_sequence(best_a, 1000)
        else:
            a_to_polish = best_a.copy()
        a_pol, sc_pol = _coord_descent(
            a_to_polish,
            final_coord_dl,
            max_cycles=100000,
            seed=12345,
            verbose=False
        )
        _add_candidate(records, a_pol)
        _add_candidate(records, best_a)

        # One tiny subgradient pass if time remains.
        if time.monotonic() < final_coord_dl - 5:
            a_sub2, sc_sub2 = _subgradient_smooth(
                a_pol,
                final_coord_dl,
                max_iters=8000 if a_pol.size <= 1200 else 3000,
                alpha=1.0,
                peaks_power=9.0,
                top_k=32 if a_pol.size <= 1200 else 20,
                verbose=False
            )
            _add_candidate(records, a_sub2)

    # 7) Verify a handful of best candidates with the official evaluator.
    final_cands = _select_diverse(records, 12)
    best_val = float("inf")
    best_seq = _prepare_final(np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64))

    for sc, a in final_cands:
        seq = _prepare_final(a)
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            val = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_seq = seq

    if not np.isfinite(best_val):
        best_seq = _prepare_final(np.ones(100, dtype=np.float64))

    print(f"Final verified score: {best_val:.7f}, length={len(best_seq)}")
    return best_seq
```
